## Summary

Swainsonine has a matching original 2017 stereochemical drawing, with manual CIP assignments kept separate from a new natural-configuration experiment. One confirmed minor citation finding routes only the exact BGC0001793:1 producer/locus references to the 2017 ICE/KY365740 source through the existing eight-field guarded consumer; identity, grades, methods, taxa and other rows remain unchanged. Separate organism and stock endpoints are not transferred. Forty-five raw occurrences deduplicate to 41 and cap at 25, with no exclusion implied. Nine assays and one graph are mechanically bound only, both full scientific read flags remain false, and source/current-authority gaps keep the review partial and needing curation.

### Taxonomic identifiers versus experimental stocks

provenance: supported. Targets: CHEBI:9367.

Retained naming context: 3803: Fabaceae (family); 5530: Metarhizium anisopliae (species); 20406: Astragalus bisulcatus (species); 20413: Astragalus hallii (species); 20416: Astragalus lentiginosus (species); 20422: Astragalus purshii (species); 20805: Oxytropis lambertii (species); 24962: Castanospermum australe (species); 47038: Astragalus falcatus (species); 89640: Ipomoea carnea (species); 89659: Ipomoea polpha (species); 90174: Astragalus didymocarpus (species); 90190: Astragalus mollissimus (species); 90201: Astragalus oxyphysus (species); 90210: Astragalus praelongus (species); 90234: Oxytropis sericea (species); 99017: Swainsona galegifolia (species); 457888: Astragalus wootonii (species); 483874: Oxytropis glabra (species); 491199: Swainsona canescens (species); 570715: Alternaria oxytropis (species); 655844: Metarhizium robertsii ARSEF 23 (strain); 1541393: Slafractonia leguminicola (species); 1769941: Astragalus whitneyi (species); 1938221: Oxytropis riparia (species); 1963369: Chaetothyriaceae sp. (species); 2014685: Astragalus flavus (species); 2607090: Ipomoea calobra (species); 2897604: Astragalus pycnostachyus (species); 3084112: Astragalus emoryanus (species). Slafractonia explicitly retains Rhizoctonia leguminicola as a synonym; Alternaria retains Embellisia and Undifilum oxytropis. ARSEF 23 is not an automatic bridge to the ARSEF 2575 intervention stock. Generic Chaetothyriaceae naming does not establish the exact ICE isolate. Forty-five raw occurrences deduplicate to forty-one and cap at twenty-five, leaving sixteen raw-only assertions. Family-level Fabaceae and exact plant-source limits remain separate. The accepted citation-only correction does not change taxon identity or source grades. Names alone do not prove occurrence, production or physical stock identity.

### native, introduced, citation and exact congener applicability

evidence: concern. Targets: CHEBI:9367.

The cited 2012 cancer paper does not support BGC1793 Chaetothyriaceae/ICE KY365740; original 2017 identity/accession context supports a narrow citation-only route, preserving all existing grades and empty methods. The separate 2017 Alternaria KY365741 and 2020 M. robertsii evidence remain distinct. Original 2017 functional endpoint reported in M. robertsii ARSEF2575 is distinct from the reference genome ARSEF23. The 2020 abstract supports M. robertsii gene-deletion evidence at broad level; exact stock/context remains a full-main gap. Forty-five LOTUS rows reduce to 41 distinct taxon/reference assertions, then 25 written with 16 omitted; four historical name duplicates share TaxId 1541393. No cap omission is an exclusion. Historical Swainsona canescens identity is positively supported by the 1979 publisher-deposited abstract. 1991 abstract supports a broad locoweed distribution; exact listed species remain unverified. 1990 Castanospermum comparator and 1995 Ipomoea Q6 aff. calobra require bounded further primary/stock adjudication, with no automatic occurrence exclusion.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- Context establishes mechanical owner/history/source equality, literal identifier routing and computed classification. Primary production, exact stock and congener claims retain their source-specific gaps. Detailed 457 assay, target and graph science remains incomplete; both full scientific owner and raw-row flags remain false. Its nine raw and emitted activities are mechanically compared only. Two separate no-data sentinels do not establish inactivity. Literal names and explicit taxon redirects do not resolve stock, congener or citation applicability. The 457 occurrence cap retains sixteen raw-only distinct assertions; the accepted citation correction affects producer/locus references only.
- All nine existing assay rows and one graph remain scientifically unreviewed; their complete bytes are mechanically bound only.
- 2020 main unavailable; most of the 45 LOTUS row primary originals and exact species panels are not completely read. Do not infer absence or universal applicability from titles or broad abstracts.
- The BGC1793 citation-only correction is accepted by ROOT and independently reviewed; it has not been applied. Fresh authority lookups remain held, independently of the original chemical panel.
- Stored/raw and reused-versioned evidence retain their original scopes and clocks. Current literal authorities remain unavailable under the approval hold. A positive original depiction does not establish an independent natural absolute-hand experiment; organism, locus, classification and history claims are separately scoped.
- All ten chemical subsections remain partial because required current literal authorities are unavailable under the approval hold. The actual retained comparison verifies stored and selected raw encodings, not natural absolute configuration or current remote payload identity. Two previously retained versioned MIBiG bodies support four selected rows at their original access clocks; eight other selected versioned rows remain unavailable remotely. Thirteen current literal roles remain unqueried or unavailable. Existing local ChEBI roles are retained separately from candidate discovery; no candidate is promoted or excluded. Whole-owner and whole-raw scientific manual flags remain false for chemistry; complete byte/document/source-join checks are mechanical. Record 457 nine assays and one graph remain outside scientific scope. Failures and approval holds do not imply chemical absence, a wrong owner, or authority disagreement. No chemical finding, replacement, merge, curation, or lifecycle execution is proposed. A matching exact source depiction does not clear the separate required current literal-authority check or establish an independent absolute-assignment experiment.
- Configured consistency only; independent scientific claim support and unrestricted remote authority refresh are not implied.
- FailedQC001 is retained for an explicit separate network-enabled retry; no artifact will be overwritten.
- Fresh QC attempt001 failed on public canonical-artifact DNS retrieval after lint and before downstream tests. Its actual exit1/log/input capture/root observation remain pinned; the selected successful canonical QC attempt002 is separately timed. No individual-review validation failure or counter reset is claimed.

review_id: 20261026T050833Z-swainsonine
title: Scientific review of swainsonine
started_at: '2026-10-26T05:06:20.741965Z'
finished_at: '2026-10-26T05:08:33.782042Z'
completion: partial
verdict: needs_curation
scientific_review: true
summary: Swainsonine has a matching original 2017 stereochemical drawing, with manual
  CIP assignments kept separate from a new natural-configuration experiment. One confirmed
  minor citation finding routes only the exact BGC0001793:1 producer/locus references
  to the 2017 ICE/KY365740 source through the existing eight-field guarded consumer;
  identity, grades, methods, taxa and other rows remain unchanged. Separate organism
  and stock endpoints are not transferred. Forty-five raw occurrences deduplicate
  to 41 and cap at 25, with no exclusion implied. Nine assays and one graph are mechanically
  bound only, both full scientific read flags remain false, and source/current-authority
  gaps keep the review partial and needing curation.

    20416: Astragalus lentiginosus (species); 20422: Astragalus purshii (species);
    20805: Oxytropis lambertii (species); 24962: Castanospermum australe (species);
    47038: Astragalus falcatus (species); 89640: Ipomoea carnea (species); 89659:

    mollissimus (species); 90201: Astragalus oxyphysus (species); 90210: Astragalus

    (species); 457888: Astragalus wootonii (species); 483874: Oxytropis glabra (species);

    655844: Metarhizium robertsii ARSEF 23 (strain); 1541393: Slafractonia leguminicola
    (species); 1769941: Astragalus whitneyi (species); 1938221: Oxytropis riparia
    (species); 1963369: Chaetothyriaceae sp. (species); 2014685: Astragalus flavus
    (species); 2607090: Ipomoea calobra (species); 2897604: Astragalus pycnostachyus

- assessment_id: R457-TAXON-SCOPE
  area: provenance
  topic: Taxonomic identifiers versus experimental stocks
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Retained naming context: 3803: Fabaceae (family); 5530: Metarhizium anisopliae
    (species); 20406: Astragalus bisulcatus (species); 20413: Astragalus hallii (species);
    20416: Astragalus lentiginosus (species); 20422: Astragalus purshii (species);
    20805: Oxytropis lambertii (species); 24962: Castanospermum australe (species);
    47038: Astragalus falcatus (species); 89640: Ipomoea carnea (species); 89659:
    Ipomoea polpha (species); 90174: Astragalus didymocarpus (species); 90190: Astragalus
    mollissimus (species); 90201: Astragalus oxyphysus (species); 90210: Astragalus
    praelongus (species); 90234: Oxytropis sericea (species); 99017: Swainsona galegifolia
    (species); 457888: Astragalus wootonii (species); 483874: Oxytropis glabra (species);
    491199: Swainsona canescens (species); 570715: Alternaria oxytropis (species);
    655844: Metarhizium robertsii ARSEF 23 (strain); 1541393: Slafractonia leguminicola
    (species); 1769941: Astragalus whitneyi (species); 1938221: Oxytropis riparia
    (species); 1963369: Chaetothyriaceae sp. (species); 2014685: Astragalus flavus
    (species); 2607090: Ipomoea calobra (species); 2897604: Astragalus pycnostachyus
    (species); 3084112: Astragalus emoryanus (species). Slafractonia explicitly retains
    Rhizoctonia leguminicola as a synonym; Alternaria retains Embellisia and Undifilum
    oxytropis. ARSEF 23 is not an automatic bridge to the ARSEF 2575 intervention
    stock. Generic Chaetothyriaceae naming does not establish the exact ICE isolate.
    Forty-five raw occurrences deduplicate to forty-one and cap at twenty-five, leaving
    sixteen raw-only assertions. Family-level Fabaceae and exact plant-source limits
    remain separate. The accepted citation-only correction does not change taxon identity
    or source grades. Names alone do not prove occurrence, production or physical
    stock identity.'

- assessment_id: S457-A-BOUNDARIES
  area: evidence
  topic: native, introduced, citation and exact congener applicability
  outcome: concern
  summary: The cited 2012 cancer paper does not support BGC1793 Chaetothyriaceae/ICE
    KY365740; original 2017 identity/accession context supports a narrow citation-only
    route, preserving all existing grades and empty methods. The separate 2017 Alternaria
    KY365741 and 2020 M. robertsii evidence remain distinct. Original 2017 functional
    endpoint reported in M. robertsii ARSEF2575 is distinct from the reference genome
    ARSEF23. The 2020 abstract supports M. robertsii gene-deletion evidence at broad
    level; exact stock/context remains a full-main gap. Forty-five LOTUS rows reduce
    to 41 distinct taxon/reference assertions, then 25 written with 16 omitted; four
    historical name duplicates share TaxId 1541393. No cap omission is an exclusion.
    Historical Swainsona canescens identity is positively supported by the 1979 publisher-deposited
    abstract. 1991 abstract supports a broad locoweed distribution; exact listed species
    remain unverified. 1990 Castanospermum comparator and 1995 Ipomoea Q6 aff. calobra
    require bounded further primary/stock adjudication, with no automatic occurrence
    exclusion.
  target_ids:
  - CHEBI:9367

    "PMID:28381497", "producer_reference": "PMID:28381497", "rationale": "The imported
    DOI:10.7150/ijbs.3882 is a cancer-bioactivity study using plant-derived swainsonine,
    not support for the Chaetothyriaceae/ICE producer and KY365740 locus. The inspected
    2017 original identifies the swainsonine-producing Ipomoea carnea endophyte in
    Chaetothyriales and explicitly associates its candidate locus with KY365740. Route
    only the producer and locus citations to that source; retain SOURCE_ASSERTION,
    CLUSTER_UNSTATED, empty locus methods, raw source reference, taxon, accession,
    coordinates and chemistry. Do not transfer the separate M. robertsii knockout/complementation
    experiments to ICE or infer a new strain identity.", "source_id": "BGC0001793:1",
    "source_reference": "DOI:10.1534/g3.117.041384"}. This correction is proposed
    only; source rows and current owner are unchanged.'

    SHA256 ce63a6aad29dd27fd23992bb5afa4f0db48726f908e26bf77acc000df2cb4496. Fail

  - Use only the existing guarded claim-reference consumer to route producer_reference
